4.7
(or
4.13
&
4.19
) provided:

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}|(\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!\!-\!\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})(\alpha^{M}_{N}(A)\Ad_{U_{N}(t)}(\alpha^{M}_{N}(B))))|

\displaystyle=0\;.

But, this is itself a consequence of Corollary
4.7
and the fact that
\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
is the scaling limit of
\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
. To see this, we observe that Corollary
4.7
implies for
\pi_{S}=\pi_{0}
(the standard Fock-space representation) that
O_{N}=\alpha^{M}_{N}(A)\Ad_{U_{N}(t)}(\alpha^{M}_{N}(B))
converges to an element
O\in\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
for any
M\in\mathbb{N}_{0}:A,B\in\mathfrak{A}_{M}
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
(uniformly on compact intervals):

\displaystyle\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\!\!\|O_{N}\!-\!\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\|

\displaystyle\!=\!\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\!\!\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(O_{N})\!-\!\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\!)\|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\!\!\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}\!(O_{N})\!-\!O\|\!+\!\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\!\!\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}\!(O_{N})\!-\!\alpha^{N}_{\infty}\!(\alpha^{K}_{N}\!(O_{K})\!)\|

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(O_{N})-O\|+\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\|O-\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(O_{K})\|

\displaystyle=0,

with
O
defined by
\pi_{0}(\alpha^{M}_{\infty}(A)\!)\sigma_{t}(\pi_{0}(\alpha^{M}_{\infty}(B)\!)\!)
, and
\pi_{0}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(O_{N})\!)\!=\!\pi_{0}(\alpha^{M}_{\infty}(A)\!)\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{t}\!(\pi_{0}(\alpha^{M}_{\infty}(B)\!)\!)
. Finally, the scaling limit construction of
\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
gives (see Lemma
3.11
):

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\!)

\displaystyle=\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(O_{K})\!),

\displaystyle\forall K

\displaystyle\in\mathbb{N}_{0},

which defines
\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
on the dense subalgebra
\bigcup_{K\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{K,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})
. The extension to
O\in\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
can be determined via the approximating sequence
\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(O_{K})
:

\displaystyle\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(O_{\infty})

\displaystyle=\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(O_{K}))=\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K}))

\displaystyle=\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(O_{N})+\lim_{K\rightarrow\infty}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(O_{N}-\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K}))

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(O_{N})\;.

which proves the result. To achieve uniform convergence in
t
on compact intervals, we need the following estimate:

\displaystyle|(\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!\!-\!\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})(O_{N})|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!|\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!(O_{N}\!-\!\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\!)|\!+\!|(\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!\!-\!\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})(\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\!)|\!+\!|\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!(\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\!-\!O_{N})|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!2\|O_{N}\!-\!\alpha^{K}_{N}(O_{K})\|\!+\!|(\omega^{(K)}_{\pm}\!-\!\omega^{(K)}_{N-K,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})(O_{K})|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!2(\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(O_{N})\!-\!O\|\!+\!\|O\!-\!\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(O_{K})\|)+\|\omega^{(K)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}-\omega^{(K)}_{N-K,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\|\|O_{K}\|.

Since, by Corollary
4.7
,
\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(O_{N})-O\|
and
\|O-\alpha^{K}_{\infty}(O_{K})\|
can be made uniformly small for
t
in a compact interval, we only need to show that this is possible for
\|\omega^{(K)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}-\omega^{(K)}_{N-K,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\|\|O_{K}\|
as well. But, this follows from the fact that
\omega^{(K)}_{N-K,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
approximates
\omega^{(K)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
in the sense of Lemma
3.11
, and provided
\|U_{K}(t)\|\leq C^{(K)}_{I}
is uniformly bounded in
t
on compact intervals
I\subset\mathbb{R}
(obviously true in the unitary case) because:

\displaystyle\|O_{K}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|U_{K}(t)\|^{2}\|A\|\|B\|.

\Box

Corollary 6.2
.

The statement of Theorem
6.1
remains valid, if
A,B\in\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{M,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
(for some
M\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
) are replaced by convergent sequences
\{A_{N}\}_{N\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}
,
\{B_{N}\}_{N\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}
in the sense of (
91
). Then

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}(\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}},\pi^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(A_{N})\sigma^{(N)}_{t}(\pi^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}((B_{N})))\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})

\displaystyle=(\Omega_{0},\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(A)\sigma_{t}(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(B))\Omega_{0})\;,

where
\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(A_{N})=A
and
\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(B_{N})=B
.

Proof.

This follows using,

\displaystyle(\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}},\pi^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(A_{N})\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})-(\Omega_{0},\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(A)\Omega_{0})

\displaystyle=(\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}},\pi^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(A_{N})\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})-(\Omega_{0},\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(A_{N}))\Omega_{0})

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt+(\Omega_{0},\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(A_{N}))\Omega_{0})-(\Omega_{0},\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(A)\Omega_{0})

\displaystyle=(\omega^{(N)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}-\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})(A_{N})+\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(A_{N})-A),

repeatedly applying the triangle inequality, and the observation that,

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\|\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(A_{N}\sigma^{(N)}_{t}(B_{N}))-A\sigma_{t}(B)\|

\displaystyle=0,

by applying Corollary
4.7
to the standard Fock-space representation
\pi_{0}
.

\Box

Virasoro correlation functions

Since the (smeared) KS approximants converge to the (smeared) Virasoro generators in the scaling limit representations
\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
using the momentum-cutoff renormalization group (possibly in combination with the wavelet renormalization group for the smearing functions) by Theorems
4.11
&
4.16
, we obtain an analogue of Theorem
6.1
for correlation functions involving the Virasoro generators or their associated unitaries, cp.
[
96
]
. A subtle difference arises from the fact that the KS approximants are only strongly operator-convergent on the Fock-space vectors with finite particle number in momentum space,
\mathfrak{F}^{\alg}_{\textup{a}}(\mathcal{D}_{\std})
, and not operator-convergent in the sense of Corollary
6.2
. Again, we state the theorem for complex fermions
\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
, but an analogous statement holds for the Majorana fermions
\mathfrak{B}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
.

Theorem 6.3

(Convergence of Virasoro correlation functions)
.

Let
s\in C^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R})
be a sufficiently regular, compactly supported orthonormal Daubechies scaling function. Given the quasi-free representations
\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
of the fermion algebra
\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
arising from the scaling limit states
\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
. Then, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
and
S
-convergent sequences of smearing functions
X_{N,p}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle N\rightarrow\infty}}{{\rightarrow}}X_{p}
,
N\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
and
p=1,...,n
with sufficient regularity as in Remark
4.18
, for example,
X_{N,p}=S^{M_{p}}_{N}(X_{p})
for
X_{p}\in\mathfrak{l}(\Lambda_{M_{p}},\mathbb{R})
, we have:

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}(\Omega_{0},\prod_{p=1}^{n}:\!(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p}))\!:\Omega_{0})

\displaystyle=(\Omega_{0},\prod_{p=1}^{n}L_{\pm}(X_{p})\Omega_{0})\;,

and similarly,

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}(\Omega_{0},\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{i:(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})):}\Omega_{0})

\displaystyle=(\Omega_{0},\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{p})}\Omega_{0})\;,

where
\Omega_{0}
is the standard Fock vacuum of
\mathfrak{F}_{\textup{a}}(\mathfrak{h}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}})
. Moreover, the finite-scale correlation functions of the scaling limit state
\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
can be approximated in terms of the renormalized finite-scale states:

\displaystyle(\Omega_{0},\prod_{p=1}^{n}:\!(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!:\Omega_{0})=\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\prod_{p=1}^{n}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})-\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!)

\displaystyle=\lim_{M\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{M,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\prod_{p=1}^{n}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})-\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(L^{(N)}_{M,\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!)

\displaystyle=\lim_{M\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N+M)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!(\prod_{p=1}^{n}(\alpha^{N}_{N+M}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}\!(X_{N,p})\!)\!-\!\omega^{(N+M)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!(\alpha^{N}_{N+M}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}\!(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!)\!)\;,

and similarly,

\displaystyle(\Omega_{0},\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{i:(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!):}\Omega_{0})=\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{iL^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})-i\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)})

\displaystyle=\lim_{M\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N)}_{M,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{iL^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})-i\omega^{(N)}_{M,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)})

\displaystyle=\lim_{M\rightarrow\infty}\omega^{(N+M)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!(\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{i\alpha^{N}_{N+M}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)-i\omega^{(N+M)}_{0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\!(\alpha^{N}_{N+M}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!)})\;,

where
\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}=\omega_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty}
.

Proof.

The statements follow from a direct application of Theorem
4.16
and Corollary
4.17
, the identity,

\displaystyle:\!(\pi_{S}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!:

\displaystyle=(\pi_{S}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)-\omega^{(N)}_{S}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!),

and the convergence, according to Lemma
3.11
, of the renormalized states
\omega^{(N)}_{M,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
at finite scales to the scaling limit
\omega^{(N)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
:

\displaystyle|(\Omega_{0},\!\Big(\prod_{p=1}^{n}\!:\!\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!:\!-\!\prod_{p=1}^{n}L_{\pm}(X_{p})\!)\!\!\Big)\Omega_{0})|

(236)

\displaystyle\!\leq\!\sum^{n}_{q=1}|(\Omega_{0},\!\Big(\!\!\prod_{p=q+1}^{n}\!\!\!\!:\!\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}\!(L^{(N)}_{\pm}\!(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!:\!\!\Big)\!\Big(\!\!:\!\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}\!(L^{(N)}_{\pm}\!(X_{N,q})\!)\!)\!:\!-L_{\pm}(X_{q})\!\!\Big)\!\Big(\!\prod_{p=1}^{q-1}L_{\pm}(X_{p})\!\!\Big)\Omega_{0})|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!\sum^{n}_{q=1}\Big\|\!\!\prod_{p=q+1}^{n}\!\!\!\!:\!\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}\!(L^{(N)}_{\pm}\!(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!:\!\Omega_{0}\Big\|\Big\|\!\Big(\!\!:\!\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}\!(X_{N,q})\!)\!)\!:\!-L_{\pm}(X_{q})\!\!\Big)\!\Big(\prod_{p=1}^{q-1}\!L_{\pm}(X_{p})\!\!\Big)\Omega_{0}\Big\|,

and similarly,

\displaystyle|(\Omega_{0},\Big(\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{i:\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!):}\!-\!\prod_{p=1}^{n}e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{p})}\!\Big)\Omega_{0})|

(237)

\displaystyle\!\leq\!\sum^{n}_{q=1}|(\Omega_{0},\!\Big(\!\!\prod_{p=q+1}^{n}\!\!\!e^{i:\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!):}\!\Big)\!\Big(e^{i:\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,q})\!)\!):}\!-e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{q})}\!\Big)\!\Big(\prod_{p=1}^{q-1}e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{p})}\!\Big)\Omega_{0})|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!\sum^{n}_{q=1}\Big\|\!\!\prod_{p=q+1}^{n}\!\!\!e^{i:\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!):}\Omega_{0}\Big\|\Big\|\!\Big(e^{i:\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,q})\!)\!):}\!-\!e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{q})}\!\Big)\!\Big(\prod_{p=1}^{q-1}e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{p})}\!\Big)\Omega_{0}\Big\|

\displaystyle\!\leq\!\sum^{n}_{q=1}\!\Big\|\!\Big(e^{i:\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,q})\!)\!):}\!-\!e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{q})}\!\Big)\!\Big(\prod_{p=1}^{q-1}e^{iL_{\pm}(X_{p})}\!\Big)\Omega_{0}\Big\|,

In the case of correlation functions of Virasoro generators, we need two additional observation: First,
\|\prod_{p=1}^{n}\!:\!\!(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}(\alpha^{N}_{\infty}(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p})\!)\!)\!\!:\!\Omega_{0}\|
can be bounded uniformly in
N
in terms of
\|\prod_{p=1}^{n}L_{\pm}(X_{p})\Omega_{0}\|
for sufficiently regular
X_{p}
as powers of the approximants
:\!(\pi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}\circ\alpha^{N}_{\infty})(L^{(N)}_{\pm}(X_{N,p}))\!:
are uniformly bounded (in
N
) by
L_{\pm}(X_{p})
. Second,
\prod_{p=1}^{n}L_{\pm}(X_{p}))\Omega_{0}
is given by a convergent sum (depending on the regularity of
X_{p}
) of Fock-space vectors with finite particle number in momentum space.

\Box

Remark 6.4

(Mixed correlation functions)
.

Theorem
6.1
&
6.3
can be combined to obtain convergent expressions for mixed correlation functions, i.e. those containing insertions of operators in
\mathfrak{A}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
respectively
\mathfrak{B}^{(\pm)}_{\infty,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm}}
, Virasoro generators, WZW currents and their associated unitaries.
